We must have been cuffed in place the best part of an hour before they got down to our level. A team of men in black with guns come in through the doors. Black cover-all's and balaclavas; black belts with cuffs and batons and stuff dangling and Police in big letters on their backs and blue square police badges on their chests.

They walked around taking notes of our collar names and the colour of the tops at our feet and our precise locations. When they got to me the guy stopped and put a hand up and shouted "here's one, Silver, Sandy."

He was joined by two others and between them they released me and cuffed my hands behind me with a 'Plasti-cuff'; then threaded my top through the back of my collar so that it would stay with me.

Once they had everybody in the lab released and then similarly cuffed they took us all into the corridor and along the central plaza to the Courtyard; but separated me off and took me up to the conference room on level six.

They had given no indication that they knew who I really was.

In the conference room sat around the main table were nine of the suits including John Smith, Candy and Gareth; they were still suited but had their jackets removed and they were all plasti-cuffed with their hands behind them.

Barry and Carl were there also, naked; unfortunately in the seat I was put in I couldn't see below Carl's waist; in his lycra shorts he certainly gave the impression of being well worth seeing so I was feeling cheated. But I could see Barry; his sheer girth was frightening!

There were police with guns stood either side of each door and in each corner of the room; all had their faces covered but I didn't think any of them were Dax.

A few minutes of silence passed and then Gold and Bronze were escorted in, also naked and cuffed. Gold looked absolutely gorgeous; walking tall and proud, almost regal; she is the first black girl I have ever seen naked and she is beautiful; her skin far lighter than I imagined and it glowed. However, obviously, it was Debbie that grabbed all the attention; I guess I could have just stood up and walked out and nobody would have noticed, the coppers were so transfixed on her amazing body.

They were seated along from me. Still none of us said a word, we all just looked at each other wondering what was going on, and looking to John Smith to do or say something but he didn't. His face was red and he was sweating.

Another copper came in and I recognised him as Dax immediately; just the way he moved and his build and when he spoke he confirmed it. "Okay guys, all staff accounted for and secure. Quick debrief then onto phase three; follow me" and he turned and headed out; all the Police following him. At the door he stopped, letting his colleagues pass him, and turned and said out loud "Don't any of you do anything stupid, don't even leave your seats; we will be checking" and followed his men out.

Gold started first "John, just what the hell is going on here, what's with the Police?"

"Don't worry Macy, the Police are just doing their jobs, they won't find anything and they will leave."

She replied to him "Won't find anything? What is there to find, we are a scientific research and production facility, a Government one at that; why would the Police want to raid a Government lab working on a secret Defence project?"

"It must be a mix up or something, or maybe they suspect fraud or an insider threat or something, I don't know but it will sort itself out, don't you worry."

Candy couldn't contain herself anymore and burst out with "Bollocks John, we've been bust, they must know all about it to be here at all; and I bet you it was that fucking bitch there (nodding at me) who led them here."

Everybody was looking at me.

"What are you talking about Candy? I'm a computer scientist, I just work here; and besides, just where is here? And why would I lead any Police here? We make stuff, electronic stuff; what the hell about that would interest the Police? You've just got it in for me haven't you like you're jealous and spiteful or something."

She continued "Believe me John, I just know it, Sandy is a spy or something and somehow she has done us in."

He just shook his head in resignation "I don't know Candy; I don't see how she could have, she hasn't a clue where this facility is and has only just became privy to the full component and it's intended capabilities; in fact she has done more than anyone to help progress the project. It's just as likely to be you that led them here the way you have behaved recently. What I do know is that one of us sat around this table, or one of the three missing suits; will be waking up at the bottom of the sea with cement boots fitted when I find out who the mole is."

There was a brief silence whilst this sank in and then Gold spoke again "So tell us John, for the benefit of the Podium that has served you faithfully and been dragged unknown into whatever this is; what is this all about?"

"We are making something that when distributed, and sold, and installed in a Nations infrastructure; would be capable of bringing that Nation down at the flick of a switch."

"But John" Gold continued, sweating and stammering now, "We are working for the Government, for Defence, isn't this what they want?"

"Yes Macy, it is, exactly what they want and are paying an absolute fortune for; but I never once said it was the British Government we were working for."

Carl spoke up "I take it all you suits were fully aware of all this then? And just led the rest of us into becoming traitors?"

There was no response but their faces said it all, they were all in it.

I added "Candy, if it was you that led the Police here, thank you, regardless of what happens to me or any of us, you have done the right thing."

"It was not me!" she shouted. "John, believe me, it was not me I have been totally loyal to you, I don't know if it was that bitch but it was not me!"

The double doors banged open and in strode Commander Belper, no mask, no face covering, no gun; just him, suited and in a black trench coat looking every inch like the spy he is. What a lovely sight for sore eyes.

He was followed by masked police with guns, one of them Dax.

"Ladies and Gentlemen, thank you for your patience. We are now going to take you away one at a time and hold some short sharp dynamic interviews before relocating you to your new long-term accommodation."

He walked slowly around us until he was stood directly behind Candy. He helped her out of her chair and said to the copper behind him "Sergeant, please take Candy out of here and get her back to London; she shouldn't have to breathe the same air as these treacherous terrorists a second more. Candy, thanks also for getting Mr Smith, or should I say Jonathon Desmond-Smyth, to admit his knowledge and part in all this; makes things much easier."

"John, John, it wasn't me, honest, it wasn't; this is some sort of set up!" She was screaming as she was taken out, no doubt just to be interviewed first.

"Don't you worry Candy" the Commander added, "I promised you a safe house and a new identity; and a Queen's pardon; you did the right thing, you are safe."

He had played my request left on the post-it note at the Waterloo perfectly.

Looking the Commander straight in the face John Smith said quietly "I do not believe for one moment that Candy led you here, she is totally loyal to me. You will have to try something better than that."

"Yes" he replied, "She said she had always been totally loyal, too loyal in fact; had it not been for the way you treated her in a conference here; and then the things you did to her in your office; she would no doubt have remained loyal and you may very well have succeeded here. Brought down by your own Dick and the spite of a woman."

"That fucking bitch; hide her well but know this, she is dead."

**56. A Bondage Bonus**

That night I was given a room in the Savoy and spent the evening drinking on the strand. I was a very rich young lady, worth over £10million already before my nineteenth birthday, either of them!

The following morning I was back at the office for a full debrief with the Commander but would be returning for four more nights in the Savoy before they moved me to my new apartment, just in case.

The Commander explained that Operation Dark Cloud had been a complete success; the threat had been totally neutralised and the people behind it identified.

It was a group of extremely powerful American businessmen; they wanted to make the USA all powerful and had identified that Britain and Europe posed the biggest future financial threat, followed by the Chinese.

They had plotted to take Europe back to the dark ages, make us a third world country; and then point the blame at the Chinese and war would follow putting China back generations also.

In the new world their individual positions of power and wealth would dramatically increase effectively making them Gods.

Special Forces operatives were now tasked with making these people disappear.

The suits were still being detained and would all be prosecuted; everybody else was being debriefed and released. They were all getting to keep their wages but that was it; and all could be called as witnesses but it would be in a closed court.

MI6 were taking the facility over as a new Military Intelligence HQ. It was built within an old quarry in Essex which is now covered with a Manor House and walled estate.

He wanted me to go back and finish the project; to hand-pick who I wanted from the staff there. The theory had been good, frighteningly good; MI6 wanted it not only as a weapon for the future but also to analyse it and create a defence to it. I explained I could do that also; they were paying me well enough.

They had bought me, with my own money and in my name, a penthouse suite overlooking the Thames by Blackfriars. I was only staying in the Savoy whilst they transferred my stuff over again. It was a good investment and a fantastic place to live. The remaining £5.5million and my wages from the facility were in my new bank account.

I wouldn't be named in the honours list, but I was to receive the Queens Medal; the youngest ever to do so.

Commander Belper introduced me to one of my concierge's and escorted me up to my suite. It was breath-taking, and brand new.

He showed me into the hallway and then the kitchen-dining area and the lounge and the tremendous view from the balcony.

He showed me my master bedroom which already had my special bed set up and linked to my computer system; and explained that the other two bedrooms were the same size and also had balconies.

He paused in the doorway on his way out and said "Take the rest of the month off, then report to the office at nine. You will find I have left you a special bonus in your spare bedrooms; keep them also until you come back, but bring them back with you; they are only on loan but are yours to do with as you please; you have earned it; thank you" and he was gone.

I was so excited, I do love surprises! I had no idea what they could be; clearly not a car or anything like that, not up here. But something special? One of the special chairs from the facility maybe? No I could hardly take them back to the office with me. I had no idea.

To keep the surprise alive longer I poured myself a large glass of red and relaxed on the balcony determined to work out what my bonus items could be.

Eventually giving up I had to go and look.

Oh my God! There, in the first bedroom I come to was Claire; naked, cuffed spread-eagle to the bed, gagged and blind-folded.

I ran to the next bedroom and Yes! My beautiful, wonderful, masterful and similarly naked and restrained Dax; and looking at his magnificent erection he was ready to serve.

I so love being a Bondagent, what a party!

END

Epilogue

Sandy posted her finished story "Dian Taylor - Oh My God" on the Utopiastories.com site and was worried by the first few critical review comments; one said it was written more like a play than a story; another said it wasn't bad considering it clearly wasn't written in the authors first language!

Fortunately subsequent comments were very flattering and this has motivated Sandy to continue writing as GT Stokes.

Using her IT skills she hacked into the Utopia site and traced the authors of all her comments; the "more like a play" comment was actually Doc taking the piss because Sandy had played out parts of the story with her in her apartment; the "wasn't written in the authors first language" comment she struggled with but eventually traced it back to MI6 and Commander Belper; he was really taking the piss and this showed that he was still monitoring her and her flat because how else would he know she had posted this story as GT Stokes?

All of the other comments traced back to genuine users of the Utopia site; people that gave real and fair critical comments. Some of them had fantastic names like DarksideDora, Squidgy Chick, River Juices; others more genuine sounding names. Sandy considered setting up a virus that would send a picture of a Dick to anyone grading her stories below a four but decided against this; just setting it up to always grade her as a five instead!

As a Bondagent Sandy delivered everything Commander Belper wished for at the facility, and is now, aged twenty three, a senior field operative in MI6 and based in the London HQ.

She is wealthy beyond her dreams and is now driven by adventure and risk alone. Well, Bondage and sex first and then adventure and risk alone. The money is just a bonus.

She is in a long-term but no strings attached relationship with Debbie (Bronze); is still fucking Dax; and has a very good Doctor.

Sandy is currently on deployment assisting head of Defence staff at the Whitehouse; sharing the secrets the President doesn't want shared with MI6. She is being watched; but that is another story.

The twelve suits all received life sentences at her Majesty's pleasure, no parole.

An attempt was made on Candy's life whilst in prison. She has now been released, provided a new identity, and sold as a sex slave into a desert tribe in the Middle East.

The two pleasure chairs have been removed from the facility. One now sits overlooking the Thames in a Penthouse adjacent to Blackfriars; the other sits in the private office of the head of scientific research at MI6 HQ, London. Both chairs are used frequently.

Sandy kept her collar.

**The End**
